<table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 100%; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"><tr style="vertical-align: top">
<td style="width: 0"/><td style="width: 1in"><b>Item 8.01</b></td><td style="text-align: justify"><b>Other Events.</b></td></tr></table>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">On February 25, 2025, Lucid Group,&#160;Inc. (&#8220;<b><i>Lucid</i></b>&#8221;
or the &#8220;<b><i>Company</i></b>&#8221;) registered for resale up to (i)&#160;100,000 shares of the Company&#8217;s Series&#160;A Convertible
Preferred Stock, par value $0.0001 per share (the &#8220;<b><i>Series&#160;A Convertible Preferred Stock</i></b>&#8221;), (ii)&#160;297,567,387
shares of the Company&#8217;s Class&#160;A Common Stock, par value $0.0001 per share (&#8220;<b><i>Common Stock</i></b>&#8221;), which
may be issued upon conversion of the Series&#160;A Convertible Preferred Stock as of December&#160;31, 2024, (iii)&#160;75,000 shares
of the Company&#8217;s Series&#160;B Convertible Preferred Stock, par value $0.0001 per share (the &#8220;<b><i>Series&#160;B Convertible
Preferred Stock</i></b>&#8221;), (iv)&#160;177,103,144 shares of Common Stock, which may be issued upon conversion of the Series&#160;B
Convertible Preferred Stock as of December&#160;31, 2024, and (v)&#160;396,188,386 shares of Common Stock, pursuant to a registration
statement and a related prospectus supplement filed by the Company with the Securities and Exchange Commission.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company is filing a copy of the legal opinion
and consent of Skadden, Arps, Slate, Meagher&#160;&amp; Flom LLP as Exhibit&#160;5.1 to this Current Report on Form&#160;8-K to add such
exhibit to the Company&#8217;s Registration Statement on Form&#160;S-3ASR (File No.&#160;333-282677).</p>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&nbsp;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">Ladies and Gentlemen:</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&nbsp;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-indent: 1in">We have acted as special United States counsel to
Lucid Group,&nbsp;Inc., a Delaware corporation (the &ldquo;Company&rdquo;), in connection with the resale by the selling stockholder identified
in Schedule A hereto (the &ldquo;Selling Stockholder&rdquo;) of up to (i)&nbsp;100,000 shares of the Company&rsquo;s Series&nbsp;A Convertible
Preferred Stock, par value $0.0001 per share (the &ldquo;Series&nbsp;A Convertible Preferred Stock&rdquo;), (ii)&nbsp;297,567,387 shares
of the Company&rsquo;s Class&nbsp;A Common Stock (the &ldquo;Series&nbsp;A Conversion Shares&rdquo;), par value $0.0001 per share (&ldquo;Common
Stock&rdquo;), which may be issued upon conversion of the Series&nbsp;A Convertible Preferred Stock as of December&nbsp;31, 2024, (iii)&nbsp;75,000
shares of the Company&rsquo;s Series&nbsp;B Convertible Preferred Stock, par value $0.0001 per share (the &ldquo;Series&nbsp;B Convertible
Preferred Stock&rdquo;, and together with the Series&nbsp;A Convertible Preferred Stock, the &ldquo;Convertible Preferred Stock&rdquo;),
(iv)&nbsp;177,103,144 shares of Common Stock, which may be issued upon conversion of the Series&nbsp;B Convertible Preferred Stock as
of December&nbsp;31, 2024 (the &ldquo;Series&nbsp;B Conversion Shares&rdquo;, and together with the Series&nbsp;A Conversion Shares, the
 &ldquo;Conversion Shares&rdquo;), and (v)&nbsp;396,188,386 shares of Common Stock (the &ldquo;Secondary Shares&rdquo;). We have been advised
that (i)&nbsp;the Series&nbsp;A Convertible Preferred Stock was issued pursuant to a subscription agreement (the &ldquo;Series&nbsp;A
Subscription Agreement&rdquo;), dated as of March&nbsp;24, 2024, between the Company and Ayar Third Investment Company (&ldquo;Ayar&rdquo;),
(ii)&nbsp;the Series&nbsp;B Convertible Preferred Stock was issued pursuant to a subscription agreement, dated as of August&nbsp;4, 2024,
between the Company and Ayar (the &ldquo;Series&nbsp;B Subscription Agreement&rdquo;), and (iii)&nbsp;the Secondary Shares were issued
pursuant to a subscription agreement, dated as of October&nbsp;16, 2024, between the Company and Ayar (the &ldquo;Secondary Shares Subscription
Agreement&rdquo;, and together with the Series&nbsp;A Subscription Agreement and Series&nbsp;B Subscription Agreement, the &ldquo;Subscription
Agreements&rdquo;).</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&nbsp;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"></P>

    <DIV STYLE="margin-top: 12pt; margin-bottom: 6pt; border-bottom: Black 1pt solid"><P STYLE="margin: 0pt">&nbsp;</P></DIV>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&nbsp;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">Lucid Group,&nbsp;Inc.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">February&nbsp;25, 2025</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">Page&nbsp;2</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&nbsp;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-indent: 1in">This opinion letter is being furnished in accordance
with the requirements of Item 601(b)(5)&nbsp;of Regulation S-K under the Securities Act of 1933 (the &ldquo;Securities Act&rdquo;).</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&nbsp;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-indent: 1in">In rendering the opinions stated herein, we have
examined and relied upon the following:</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&nbsp;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-indent: 1in">(a)&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;the
registration statement on Form&#8239;S-3ASR (File No.&#8239;333-282677) of the Company relating to Common Stock, preferred stock and other
securities of the Company filed on October&#8239;16, 2024 with the Securities and Exchange Commission (the &ldquo;Commission&rdquo;) under
the Securities Act, allowing for delayed offerings pursuant to Rule&#8239;415 of the General Rules&#8239;and Regulations under the Securities
Act (the &ldquo;Rules&#8239;and Regulations&rdquo;), including the information deemed to be a part of the registration statement pursuant
to Rule&#8239;430B of the Rules&#8239;and Regulations (such registration statement being hereinafter referred to as the &ldquo;Registration
Statement&rdquo;);</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#8239;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-indent: 1in">(b)&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;the
prospectus, dated October&#8239;16, 2024 (the &ldquo;Base Prospectus&rdquo;), which forms a part of and is included in the Registration
Statement;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#8239;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-indent: 1in"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">(c)&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;the
prospectus supplement, dated February&#8239;</FONT>25, 2025 (together with the Base Prospectus, the &ldquo;Prospectus&rdquo;), relating
to the offering of the Convertible Preferred Stock, the Secondary Shares and the Conversion Shares, in the form filed with the Commission
pursuant to Rule&#8239;424(b)&#8239;of the Rules&#8239;and Regulations;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#8239;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-indent: 1in">(d)&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;executed
copies of the Subscription Agreements;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#8239;</P>

date hereof (the &ldquo;Secretary&rsquo;s Certificate&rdquo;);</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#8239;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"></P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-indent: 1in">(f)&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;a

(the &ldquo;Amended and Restated Certificate of Incorporation&rdquo;);</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-indent: 1in">(g)&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;&#8239;a

certified pursuant to the Secretary&rsquo;s Certificate; and</P>

October&#8239;11, 2024, certified pursuant to the Secretary&rsquo;s Certificate.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#8239;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-indent: 1in">We have also examined originals or copies, certified
or otherwise identified to our satisfaction, of such records of the Company and such agreements, certificates and receipts of public officials,
certificates of officers or other representatives of the Company, the Selling Stockholder and others, and such other documents as we have
deemed necessary or appropriate as a basis for the opinions stated below, including the facts and conclusions set forth in the Secretary&rsquo;s
Certificate and the factual representations and warranties contained in the Subscription Agreements.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#8239;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-indent: 1in">In our examination, we have assumed the genuineness
of all signatures, including electronic signatures, the legal capacity and competency of all natural persons, the authenticity of all
documents submitted to us as originals, the conformity to original documents of all documents submitted to us as facsimile, electronic,
certified or photocopied copies, and the authenticity of the originals of such copies. With respect to our opinion set forth in paragraph
1 below, we have assumed that (i)&#8239;the Company received the consideration for the Convertible Preferred Stock and the Secondary Shares
set forth in the applicable Subscription Agreements and board resolutions and (ii)&#8239;each issuance of the Convertible Preferred Stock
and the Secondary Shares has been registered in the Company&rsquo;s share registry. As to any facts relevant to the opinions stated herein
that we did not independently establish or verify, we have relied upon statements and representations of officers and other representatives
of the Company and the Selling Stockholder and others and of public officials, including the facts and conclusions set forth in the Amended
and Restated Certificate of Incorporation and the Secretary&rsquo;s Certificate and the factual representations and warranties set forth
in the Subscription Agreements.</P>

will be validly issued, fully paid and nonassessable.</P>
